[PR#679](https://github.com/bbc/psammead/pull/679) Bump version number |\npackages/README.md:73:**NOTE**: if you run into issues with CSS not being applied to your components, it is likely that there is a duplicate `styled-components` dependency somewhere in your packages. You can try running [`npm dedupe`](https://www.styled-components.com/docs/faqs#duplicated-module-in-node_modules) in most cases, or [`lerna bootstrap --hoist`](https://www.styled-components.com/docs/faqs#usage-with-lerna) in monorepo setups such as Psammead's.
